**Report Date:** 2026-01-27 22:04:06

---

## Executive Summary

This report demonstrates Layer 1 (Proof Engine) of the Institutional Validation Framework.
It provides undeniable proof that Northstar makes money after costs with strict temporal discipline.

**Key Findings:**
- Northstar delivered **+11.06%** cumulative return
- NIFTY delivered **+1242.58%** cumulative return
- Outperformance: **-1231.52%**
- Sharpe Ratio: **0.73** vs NIFTY **-0.85**
- Win Rate: **0.2%** (positive return months)
